44	const NO_SIT_NPC_IDS = new Set([638, 656, 670, 678, 679, 680, 681, 682, 683, 684]);
45	
46	/** FindGoodRestingSpot 扫描期成员判定（:53223 CanBeSatOnForNPCs，无 style 门——
47	 *  style 门在 TryForcingSitting，见 canSitAt） */
48	export function isSitTile(tileId: number): boolean {
49	  return CHAIR_TILE_IDS.has(tileId) || TOILET_TILE_IDS.has(tileId);
50	}
51	
52	/** AI_007_TryForcingSitting 坐下门（NPC.cs:53269-53275）：椅/马桶格 + 椅子
53	 *  style 27 排除（type 15 且 frameY∈[1080,1098]——上格 1080 / 下格 1098 两帧
54	 *  恰好夹住该样式行带；马桶无此门） */
55	export function canSitAt(tileId: number, frameY: number): boolean {
56	  if (TOILET_TILE_IDS.has(tileId)) return true;
57	  if (!CHAIR_TILE_IDS.has(tileId)) return false;
58	  return frameY < 1080 || frameY > 1098;
59	}
60	
61	export interface SitSpot { x: number; y: number; }
62	
63	/** AI_007_FindGoodRestingSpot 夜椅扫描（NPC.cs:53195-53262）：
64	 *  - 自 home 向下找地面（SolidOrSlopedTileOrPlatform = 实心非 top 或平台，:53186）
65	 *  - ±7 列 × 行 {+2,0,-2,-4,-6}（num2/num4/num3/num6）扫可坐格；上格(frameY%40==0)
66	 *    仅当 row+1<=floorY+2 收（:53224），下格恒收
67	 *  - 取 |dx|+|dy| 最小（严格 <，先到先得，:53226-53233）
68	 *  - 归一（:53247-53252）：下格(frameY%40!=0)先 -1 再 +2、上格直接 +2 → 返回
69	 *    {x, y}=落座地面行（椅上格 +2 = 地面行）。无椅 → null（白天原版直接返回
70	 *    home 地面（:53209-53212），由调用方不调本扫描） */
71	export function findChairSpot(st: TileStore, homeX: number, homeY: number): SitSpot | null {
72	  let fx = homeX, fy = homeY;
73	  if (fx < 0 || fy < 0) return null;                       // :53200-53203 home 无效
74	  while (fy < st.h - 20 && !(st.isSolid(fx, fy) || st.isPlatform(fx, fy))) fy++;
75	  let best: { x: number; y: number; d: number; frameY: number } | null = null;
76	  for (let i = fx - 7; i <= fx + 7; i++) {
77	    for (let r = fy + 2; r >= fy - 6; r -= 2) {
78	      if (!st.inBounds(i, r)) continue;
79	      const ti = st.idx(i, r);
80	      if (!isSitTile(st.type[ti])) continue;               // tile.active() 同义（type!=0）
81	      const topFrame = st.frameY[ti] % 40 === 0;
82	      if (topFrame && !(r + 1 <= fy + 2)) continue;        // :53224
83	      const d = Math.abs(i - fx) + Math.abs(r - fy);       // :53226
84	      if (!best || d < best.d) best = { x: i, y: r, d, frameY: st.frameY[ti] };
85	    }
86	  }
87	  if (!best) return null;                                  // :53255-53257
88	  let ry = best.y;
89	  if (best.frameY % 40 !== 0) ry--;                        // :53249-53251 下格 → 上移一行
90	  ry += 2;                                                 // :53252 → 地面行
91	  return { x: best.x, y: ry };
92	}
93	
94	/** 坐姿朝向（:53284 / PlayerSittingHelper.cs:231 同式）：frameX!=0（椅 flip 档
95	 *  +18）→ 1 朝右，否则 -1 朝左 */
96	export function chairFacing(frameX: number): number {
97	  return frameX !== 0 ? 1 : -1;
98	}
99	
100	/** 坐姿锚（:53285-53286）：Bottom = (x*16 + 8 + 2*dir, y*16)——脚底贴地面行顶，
101	 *  比玩家坐姿（PlayerSittingHelper 座面上 16px）更低，即原版城镇 NPC"陷坐"观感 */
102	export function sittingAnchor(x: number, y: number, dir: number): { x: number; y: number } {
103	  return { x: x * TILE + 8 + 2 * dir, y: y * TILE };
104	}

106	/** 入座时长（:53283）：ai[1] = 900 + rand(10800) */
107	export const SIT_TIME_BASE = 900;
108	export const SIT_TIME_RAND = 10800;
109	/** 时限起身后的转向冷却（:54707 localAI[3] = 30 + rand(60)；本仓由 wanderT 承接） */
110	export const SIT_STAND_WANDER_BASE = 30;
111	export const SIT_STAND_WANDER_RAND = 60;

113	/** 坐姿帽 Y 修正链 num6（Main.cs:26965-26987：ai[0]==5 时默认 −4，例外表如下；
114	 *  550/588 原版显式列 −4 = 默认值，不重复入表） */
115	export const SITTING_HAT_OFFSET_Y_DEFAULT = -4;
116	export const SITTING_HAT_OFFSET_Y: Record<number, number> = {
117	  38: -8, 124: -2, 108: -6, 178: -6, 637: -12, 663: -8,
118	};
119	/** 坐姿帽 Y 修正取值（Renderer.drawPartyHat 消费） */
120	export function sittingHatY(id: number): number {
121	  return SITTING_HAT_OFFSET_Y[id] ?? SITTING_HAT_OFFSET_Y_DEFAULT;
122	}

124	// ===================== shimmerTransparency（NPC.cs:6112，0..1）=====================
125	export const SHIMMER_ST_RAMP = 0.01;      // 浸微光 +0.01/t（:92469）
126	export const SHIMMER_ST_TRANSFORM = 0.9;  // >0.9 触发 GetShimmered（:92471）
127	export const SHIMMER_ST_RISE = 0.89;      // 城镇转化置 0.89（:92568）
128	export const SHIMMER_ST_DECAY_RISE = 1 / 60;  // ai[0]=25 上升态 −1/60/t（:53625）
129	export const SHIMMER_ST_DECAY_HIT = 0.1;      // justHit −0.1/t（:92484）
130	export const SHIMMER_ST_DECAY_IDLE = 0.001;   // 常规 −0.001/t（:92492）
131	
132	/** shimmerTransparency 每 tick 推进（赋值时序 NPC.cs:92468-92497 + :53625）：
133	 *  浸微光 +0.01 cap 1（rise 态抑制——GetShimmered 已摘除 buff 353（:92570-92573），
134	 *  上升态内 shimmering 恒 false，st 走 −1/60 衰减）；非浸按 justHit/rise/常规
135	 *  三档衰减，下限 0。>0.9 的转化触发由调用方判（见 fixedUpdate 微光块） */
136	export function shimmerTransparencyNext(
137	  st: number, shimmering: boolean, justHit: boolean, rise: boolean,
138	): number {
139	  if (shimmering && !rise) return Math.min(1, st + SHIMMER_ST_RAMP);
140	  const rate = justHit ? SHIMMER_ST_DECAY_HIT : rise ? SHIMMER_ST_DECAY_RISE : SHIMMER_ST_DECAY_IDLE;
141	  return Math.max(0, st - rate);
142	}
143	
144	/** 消费①：本体渐显/渐隐（GetAlpha NPC.cs:94874-94878：CurrentlyShimmerTransparent
145	 *  → 四通道 ×(1−shimmerTransparency)；Renderer 本体 alpha 用） */
146	export function shimmerBodyAlpha(shimmerTransparency: number): number {
147	  return 1 - shimmerTransparency;
148	}
149	
150	/** 消费②：160 松露人微光态 Glow RGB 衰减（Main.cs:25770-25776：case 160 专属，
151	 *  209 无此档）——'lighter' 加性混合下 tint RGB ×(1−st) ≡ alpha ×(1−st) */
152	export function shimmerGlowAlpha(baseAlpha: number, shimmerTransparency: number): number {
153	  return shimmerTransparency > 0 ? baseAlpha * (1 - shimmerTransparency) : baseAlpha;
154	}
155	
156	// ===================== ai[0]=25 微光上升态（NPC.cs:53594-53682）=====================
157	export const SHIMMER_RISE_VELOCITY_TICK = 30;   // ai[1]≥30 起 st 衰减 + 上浮速度（:53621）
158	export const SHIMMER_RISE_END_TICK = 75;        // ai[1]≥75 且 st≤0 → 转化收尾（:53653）
159	export const SHIMMER_RISE_PAUSE = 30;           // 远距落点后 ai[2]=30 暂停（:53604）
160	export const SHIMMER_RISE_TELEPORT_DIST = 560;  // 落点位移 ≥560px 才暂停+发送粒子（:56001）
161	
162	/** Utils.Remap（Utils.cs:313，clamped 默认 true）：fromMin..fromMax 归一后
163	 *  线性映到 toMin..toMax（区间外钳端值） */
164	export function remapClamped(
165	  v: number, fromMin: number, fromMax: number, toMin: number, toMax: number,
166	): number {
167	  const t = fromMax === fromMin ? 0 : (v - fromMin) / (fromMax - fromMin);
168	  return toMin + (Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t))) * (toMax - toMin);
169	}
170	
171	/** Main.IsItStorming（Main.cs:2978 = _shouldUseStormMusic；UpdateWindyDayState
172	 *  :12927-12960 迟滞机，阈值 _minWind 0.34/_maxWind 0.4/_minRain 0.4/_maxRain
173	 *  0.5 见 Main.cs:66137-66140）逐步推进的纯函数：无云恒 false；低雨/低风翻
174	 *  false；高雨(≥0.5)+强风(|w|≥0.4) 翻 true；中间带保持前值（迟滞）。带 prev
175	 *  逐步喂入 ≡ 原版每帧重算的全局字段。remixWorld 强制 false 分支本仓无 remix 世界 */
176	export function isItStormingNext(cloudAlpha: number, windSpeedTarget: number, prev: boolean): boolean {
177	  if (cloudAlpha === 0) return false;
178	  if (cloudAlpha < 0.4 || Math.abs(windSpeedTarget) < 0.34) return false;
179	  if (cloudAlpha >= 0.5 && Math.abs(windSpeedTarget) >= 0.4) return true;
180	  return prev;
181	}
182	
183	// ===================== Boss 击杀进度强化链（AI_007_TownEntities NPC.cs:53400-53557）=====================
184	// num2 = 伤害乘区（先于难度曲线：num = GetAttackDamage_ForTownNPC(num × num2)，
185	// 四攻击态入参 ：54897/:55169/:55319/:55466）；num3 = 攻击频率门除数（:55859
186	// num123=(int)(AttackAverageChance×num3)，钳 ≥1 后 rand(num123)==0）。两者在同一
187	// isLikeATownNPC 块内逐项累计。块内不属本函数的条目：防御档 :53401-53412
188	// （dryadWard +20/15/10 按难度——树妖结界 buff 本仓无载体，hurt 侧防御链另批）、
189	// type==209 num3÷3（:53548，attackUpdate 消费点内联）、对话中 num3×0.8（:55617，
190	// 本仓 talking 恒不入攻击态——触发门 :835 已挡，不可达登记）。
191	/** 进度强化链输入（全 bool；各字段 = 原版静态位/实例位，括注声明行+置位点） */
192	export interface TownNpcProgression {
193	  combatBookWasUsed: boolean;            // NPC.combatBookWasUsed（:6256；书 4382 使用 Player.cs:44710）
194	  combatBookVolumeTwoWasUsed: boolean;   // NPC.combatBookVolumeTwoWasUsed（:6258；书 5336 Player.cs:44723）
195	  downedSlimeKing: boolean;              // NPC.downedSlimeKing（:6270；case 50 event 11 :80217）
196	  downedBoss1: boolean;                  // NPC.downedBoss1（:6262；克眼 4 event 13 :80242）
197	  downedDeerclops: boolean;              // NPC.downedDeerclops（:6314；668 event 25 :80156）
198	  downedBoss2: boolean;                  // NPC.downedBoss2（:6264；世吞/克脑 13/14/15/266 event 14 :80258）
199	  downedBoss3: boolean;                  // NPC.downedBoss3（:6266；骷髅王 35 event 15 :80264）
200	  downedQueenBee: boolean;               // NPC.downedQueenBee（:6268；蜂后 222 event 8 :80188）
201	  hardMode: boolean;                     // Main.hardMode（肉山 113 → WorldGen.StartHardmode :80286）
202	  downedQueenSlime: boolean;             // NPC.downedQueenSlime（:6312；657 event 24 :80159）
203	  downedMechBoss1: boolean;              // NPC.downedMechBoss1（:6340；毁灭者 134 event 16 :80277）
204	  downedMechBoss2: boolean;              // NPC.downedMechBoss2（:6342；双子 125/126 event 17 :80227）
205	  downedMechBoss3: boolean;              // NPC.downedMechBoss3（:6344；Prime 127 event 18 :80270）
206	  downedPlantBoss: boolean;              // NPC.downedPlantBoss（:6280；世花 262 event 12 :80234）
207	  downedEmpressOfLight: boolean;         // NPC.downedEmpressOfLight（:6310；光女 636 event 23 :80153）
208	  downedFishron: boolean;                // NPC.downedFishron（:6286；猪鲨 370 event 7 :80150）
209	  downedGolemBoss: boolean;              // NPC.downedGolemBoss（:6282；石巨人 245 event 6 :80147）
210	  downedAncientCultist: boolean;         // NPC.downedAncientCultist（:6298；拜月教主 439 event 9 :80195）
211	  skyblockLowTiles: boolean;             // WorldGen.Skyblock.lowTiles（WorldGen.cs:3076；天空盒世界扫块率<0.1 置位 :3210-3216）
212	  tipsy: boolean;                        // NPC.tipsy（:6146；buff 25 UpdateNPC_BuffSetFlags :93179）
213	}
214	
215	/** num2/num3 进度强化链 1:1（NPC.cs:53400-53557，仅 isLikeATownNPC 块内条目）。
216	 *  返回 damageMul=num2 / chanceMul=num3。顺序敏感项：Skyblock 的 num3 折半
217	 *  （num3>1 才取 (num3+1)/2，:53542-53545，非乘法）在链中段；tipsy 在链尾
218	 *  乘算（:53555 damage×1.1 / :53556 chance×0.9）。209 的 num3÷3（:53548）
219	 *  在 Skyblock 之后、tipsy 之前——由消费点内联（对乘法项可交换，仅与 Skyblock
220	 *  折半的组合序有差；skyblockLowTiles 本仓恒 false，见 fromWorld 登记） */
221	export function townNpcProgressionBoost(p: TownNpcProgression): { damageMul: number; chanceMul: number } {
222	  let damage = 1;                        // num2 = 1f（:53400）
223	  let chance = 2;                        // num3 = 2f（:53413）
224	  if (p.combatBookWasUsed) {             // :53419-53425（lifeMax+250/防+8 留 hurt 侧）
225	    chance *= 0.8; damage += 0.25;
226	  }
227	  if (p.combatBookVolumeTwoWasUsed) {    // :53426-53432
228	    chance *= 0.8; damage += 0.25;
229	  }
230	  if (p.downedSlimeKing) {               // :53437-53442
231	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.05;
232	  }
233	  if (p.downedBoss1) {                   // :53443-53448
234	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.05;
235	  }
236	  if (p.downedDeerclops) {               // :53449-53454
237	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.1;
238	  }
239	  if (p.downedBoss2) {                   // :53455-53460
240	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.1;
241	  }
242	  if (p.downedBoss3) {                   // :53461-53466
243	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.1;
244	  }
245	  if (p.downedQueenBee) {                // :53467-53472
246	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.1;
247	  }
248	  if (p.hardMode) {                      // :53473-53478（+0.4 最大单项）
249	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.4;
250	  }
251	  if (p.downedQueenSlime) {              // :53479-53484
252	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
253	  }
254	  if (p.downedMechBoss1) {               // :53485-53490
255	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
256	  }
257	  if (p.downedMechBoss2) {               // :53491-53496
258	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
259	  }
260	  if (p.downedMechBoss3) {               // :53497-53502
261	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
262	  }
263	  if (p.downedPlantBoss) {               // :53503-53508
264	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
265	  }
266	  if (p.downedEmpressOfLight) {          // :53509-53514
267	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
268	  }
269	  if (p.downedFishron) {                 // :53515-53520
270	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
271	  }
272	  if (p.downedGolemBoss) {               // :53521-53526
273	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
274	  }
275	  if (p.downedAncientCultist) {          // :53527-53532（防 +20 最大防御项）
276	    chance *= 0.985; damage += 0.15;
277	  }
278	  if (p.skyblockLowTiles) {              // :53540-53547（netMode!=1 单机恒过）
279	    if (chance > 1) chance = (chance + 1) / 2;
280	    damage += 0.5;
281	  }
282	  if (p.tipsy) {                         // :53552-53557（链尾乘算）
283	    damage *= 1.1; chance *= 0.9;
284	  }
285	  return { damageMul: damage, chanceMul: chance };
286	}
287	
288	/** world.flags → 进度强化链输入。Boss 旗标本仓 = Game Boss 槽击杀通用记账
289	 *  `downed_<vanillaId>`（Game.ts 击败链，双子归一 125；世吞归一 13/克脑 266），
290	 *  与 Game.shopCondOk 同源键序；旧档/导入别名（downedEyeOfCthulhu/downedSkeletron/
291	 *  downedSlimeKing）双读。恒 false 两项 = 精确登记非近似：
292	 *  - skyblockLowTiles：WorldGen.cs:3210-3216 需 ScanTiles 全图活跃块率 <0.1 的
293	 *    扫描链，本仓无该计数系统（seedFlags.skyblock 只证世界类型，后期铺满 >10%
294	 *    即失格——不可拿它近似）；
295	 *  - tipsy：NPC buff 25 需城镇 NPC buff 载体（UpdateNPC_BuffSetFlags :93179），
296	 *    本仓城镇 NPC 无 buff 栏 */
297	export function townNpcProgressionFromWorld(world: {
298	  flags: Record<string, boolean>;
299	}): TownNpcProgression {
300	  const f = world.flags;
301	  return {
302	    combatBookWasUsed: !!f.combatBookWasUsed,
303	    combatBookVolumeTwoWasUsed: !!f.combatBookVolumeTwoWasUsed,
304	    downedSlimeKing: !!(f['downed_50'] || f.downedSlimeKing),
305	    downedBoss1: !!(f['downed_4'] || f.downedEyeOfCthulhu),
306	    downedDeerclops: !!f['downed_668'],
307	    downedBoss2: !!(f['downed_13'] || f['downed_266']),
308	    downedBoss3: !!(f['downed_35'] || f.downedSkeletron),
309	    downedQueenBee: !!f['downed_222'],
310	    hardMode: !!f.hardMode,
311	    downedQueenSlime: !!f['downed_657'],
312	    downedMechBoss1: !!f['downed_134'],
313	    downedMechBoss2: !!(f['downed_125'] || f['downed_126']),
314	    downedMechBoss3: !!f['downed_127'],
315	    downedPlantBoss: !!f['downed_262'],
316	    downedEmpressOfLight: !!f['downed_636'],
317	    downedFishron: !!f['downed_370'],
318	    downedGolemBoss: !!f['downed_245'],
319	    downedAncientCultist: !!f['downed_439'],
320	    skyblockLowTiles: false,
321	    tipsy: false,
322	  };
323	}
324	
325	export class TownNPC extends Entity {
326	  w = 20; h = 42;
327	  stepUp = true;
328	  stepUpHolds = true; stepUpNpc = true;   // holdsMatching + IgnoredByNpcStepUp（NPC.cs:54382 flag22 恒 true） // 原版 NPC 全体走 Collision.StepUp（NPC.cs:54382）
329	  npcKey: string; // 'guide'
330	  /** 原版 NPCID（TOWN_NPC_IDS；Renderer 经 atlas.vnpc 取 NPC_{id}.png 贴图条） */
331	  vanillaId: number;
332	  facing = 1;
333	  /** 随机名字（NPC.getNewNPCNameInner 名字池；无池类型如守卫老人为空串） */
334	  givenName = '';
335	  animT = 0;
336	  frame = 0;       // 当前贴图帧（原版 frame.Y/帧高）
337	  frameT = 0;      // 行走帧累加器（≈原版 frameCounter）
338	  wanderT = 0;
339	  home: NpcHome | null = null;
340	  /** 定点站岗（守卫老人）：门口地带是山坡+塔心竖井,白天的自由游走会坠入地牢；
341	   *  原版观感也是整日守在门口走廊 */
342	  stationary = false;
343	  /** 被绑待救援（原版为独立 NPC 类型：BoundGoblin105/BoundWizard106/BoundMechanic123/
344	   *  WebbedStylist354/GolferRescue589，本作以本标记近似；渲染按真帧走各捆绑姿态表——
345	   *  Renderer.BOUND_NPC_SHEET。原地不动，右键解救 → savedXxx 旗标置位成为正式城镇 NPC） */
346	  bound = false;
347	  // ---- NPC 开关门（原版 AI_007，NPC.cs:54478 开门 / 54243 过门关门）----
348	  private closeDoor = false;
349	  private doorX = 0; private doorY = 0;
350	  /** 白天游走但不远离 home(守卫老人:锚地牢门口走廊,防走失) */
351	  leashHome = false;
352	  /** 正在与玩家对话（原版 AI_007 :53721：talkNPC==本体 → ai[0]=0 站定、面向玩家） */

1028	  /** 当前类型的态参数(10/12/14;15 走 TOWN_SWING);hardMode 覆盖按 world.flags */
1029	  private atkParams(atkType: 0 | 1 | 2 | 3): TownShotParams | undefined {
1030	    const table = atkType === 0 ? TOWN_MELEE : atkType === 1 ? TOWN_BOW : atkType === 2 ? TOWN_MAGIC : undefined;
1031	    if (!table) return undefined;
1032	    const base = table[this.vanillaId];
1033	    if (!base) return undefined;
1034	    if (base.hardMode && this.hardModeFlag) return { ...base, ...base.hardMode } as TownShotParams;
1035	    // 633 狼人态(:54782-54785):换 proj 929 + dmg×1.5 取(int)截断(15→22)
1036	    if (this.vanillaId === 633 && this.werewolfFlag) {
1037	      return { ...base, proj: 929, dmg: Math.trunc(base.dmg * 1.5) };
1038	    }
1039	    return base;
1040	  }

1043	  /** 挥击态(15):前方矩形近战判定(:55469-55494;原版逐 tick 弧形挥击,近似中段一击) */
1044	  private swingUpdate(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks): void {
1045	    const sw = TOWN_SWING[this.vanillaId];
1046	    if (!sw) return;
1047	    const t = TOWN_ATTACK_TIME[this.vanillaId] ?? 15;
1048	    if (this.atkTick !== Math.max(1, Math.floor(t / 2))) return; // 挥击中段
1049	    const rect = sw.rect;
1050	    const rx = this.facing === 1 ? this.cx : this.cx - rect;
1051	    const ry = this.cy - rect / 2;
1052	    let dmg = sw.dmg;
1053	    let kb = sw.kb;
1054	    if (this.vanillaId === 441 && this.givenName === 'Andrew') { dmg *= 2; kb *= 2; } // 猫单特典 :55431
1055	    // num2 进度强化链(:53400-53557)先于难度曲线(:55466 同式)
1056	    const num2 = townNpcProgressionBoost(townNpcProgressionFromWorld(world)).damageMul;
1057	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
1058	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
1059	        hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean };
1060	      if (e.dead) continue;
1061	      if (!(rx < e.x + e.w && rx + rect > e.x && ry < e.y + e.h && ry + rect > e.y)) continue;
1062	      // 伤害结算(:55466):GetAttackDamage_ForTownNPC(num82*num2)——(int) 截断
1063	      if (e.hurt(getAttackDamageForTownNPC(dmg * num2, mainDifficulty(world)), this.facing * kb, 0, game)) {
1064	        game.addDamageNumber(e.x, e.y, dmg, false, '#FFD060');
1065	      }
1066	    }
1067	  }

1069	  /** 探测范围内按侧取最近可打敌(:53887-53958 近侧目标;LOS=Collision.CanHit)。
1070	   *  返回含实体 position/Size——160/663 落点弹几何按原版 Main.npc[n].position/Size 采样 */
1071	  private pickSideTarget(world: import('../world/World').World, game: GameHooks, range: number):
1072	    { cx: number; cy: number; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number } | null {
1073	    let bestL: { cx: number; cy: number; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; d: number } | null = null;
1074	    let bestR: { cx: number; cy: number; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; d: number } | null = null;
1075	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
1076	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
1077	        damage?: number; def?: { damage?: number } };
1078	      if (e.dead) continue;
1079	      // 原版扫描门(:53887):!friendly && damage>0 才算威胁(0 伤敌/友好不算)
1080	      const edmg = e.damage ?? e.def?.damage ?? 0;
1081	      if (edmg <= 0) continue;
1082	      // 中心由 x/y/w/h 推导(敌实体有 cx/cy,但勿依赖——判距门曾因 NaN 恒假失效)
1083	      const ecx = e.x + e.w / 2, ecy = e.y + e.h / 2;
1084	      const dx = ecx - this.cx;
1085	      const d = Math.hypot(dx, ecy - this.cy);
1086	      if (d >= range) continue;
1087	      if (!canHit(world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e.x, e.y, e.w, e.h)) continue;
1088	      const cand = { cx: ecx, cy: ecy, x: e.x, y: e.y, w: e.w, h: e.h, d };
1089	      if (dx < 0) { if (!bestL || d < bestL.d) bestL = cand; }
1090	      else { if (!bestR || d < bestR.d) bestR = cand; }
1091	    }
1092	    // num15 侧选择(:53960):两侧都有 → 距离近者;仅一侧 → 该侧
1093	    if (bestL && bestR) return bestL.d <= bestR.d ? bestL : bestR;
1094	    return bestL ?? bestR;
1095	  }

// 城镇 NPC 自卫攻击伤害难度缩放(NPC.cs:7041-7044 GetAttackDamage_ForTownNPC =
// ×GameDifficultyData.cs:69 TownNPCDamageMultiplier.Sample(Main.Difficulty)):
//   Journey 2 / Classic 1 / Expert 1.5 / Master 1.75(曲线无 Master 键——Sample 在
//   Expert1.5-Legendary2 区间线性插值)/ Legendary 2;
//   (int) 强转 = 向零截断(11×1.5=16.5→16,非四舍五入 17)。
// 消费点 = AI_007 四攻击态伤害入参(近战 :54897/弓 :55169/魔法 :55319/挥击 :55466)。
// 附:633 狼人变体(:54782-54785)/108 连发权重 4:2:1(:55336-55348)/
// 160·663 落点弹几何(:55349/:55367)同批评测。
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { TownNPC } from '../src/entities/TownNPC';
import { TownShot } from '../src/entities/TownShot';
import { getAttackDamageForTownNPC, GDL } from '../src/stats/ScaleStats';

interface TestEnemy {
  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; damage: number;
  hurt: () => boolean;
}

function makeGame(enemy: TestEnemy, difficulty: number, clock?: (w: World) => void) {
  const world = new World(200, 200, 100, 'town-dmg-test');
  world.difficulty = difficulty;
  for (let x = 20; x <= 180; x++) world.store.setTileSilent(x, 100, 1);
  clock?.(world);
  const shots: TownShot[] = [];
  const enemies = [enemy];
  return {
    world, shots,
    game: {
      world,
      player: { x: -9999, y: -9999, w: 0, h: 0, dead: true },
      enemies: () => enemies,
      spawnTownShot: (s: TownShot) => shots.push(s),
      spawnParticles: () => {}, playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {},
      addDamageNumber: () => {},
      critters: () => [],
    } as never,
  };
}

/** 跑到 cond 成立(每 tick 后查;攻击触发门带掷骰,给足窗口) */
function runUntil(npc: TownNPC, game: unknown, cond: () => boolean, maxT = 1500): void {
  for (let t = 0; t < maxT && !cond(); t++) npc.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game as never);
}

describe('TownNPC 攻击伤害难度缩放(GetAttackDamage_ForTownNPC)', () => {
  it('纯函数五档:Classic 1 / Expert 1.5 / Master 1.75 插值 / Legendary 2 / Journey 2', () => {
    expect(getAttackDamageForTownNPC(12, GDL.CLASSIC)).toBe(12);
    expect(getAttackDamageForTownNPC(12, GDL.EXPERT)).toBe(18);
    expect(getAttackDamageForTownNPC(12, GDL.MASTER)).toBe(21);      // 12×1.75

78	/** 纯函数:弹型 → 绘制规格(未列入走 AI_001 默认 +π/2) */
79	export function townDrawSpec(projId: number): TownDrawSpec {
80	  return TOWN_DRAW_SPEC[projId] ?? { rot: 'up', cols: 0, spinRate: 0 };
81	}
82	
83	/** 纯函数:帧号(竖排行 idx / 横向列 idx 通用)。age 由 life 差值推导(MountShot 不走
84	 *  super.fixedUpdate,故不依赖自维护计数)。 */
85	export function townFrameIndex(projId: number, age: number, ai1: number, ai2: number): number {
86	  switch (projId) {
87	    case 880: return Math.min(projFrameCount(880) - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(age)));  // AI_183 :40427 +1/t 钳末帧
88	    case 589: return Math.max(0, Math.min(4, Math.floor(ai1)));                        // ai[1]=rand5(NPC.cs:54953)
89	    case 590: {                                                                        // AI_112 :33025-33038
90	      const base = ai2 === 1 ? 3 : 0;
91	      return base + (Math.floor(age / 4) % 3);
92	    }
93	    default: return 0;                                                                 // 585/456 族 AI 不推帧
94	  }
95	}
96	
97	/** 纯函数:当前旋转角(不含 mirror 的翻转位——翻转由 draw 侧按 vx 判) */
98	export function townRotation(mode: TownRotMode, vx: number, vy: number, rotAcc: number,
99	  age: number, spinRate: number): number {
100	  switch (mode) {
101	    case 'up': return Math.atan2(vy, vx) + Math.PI / 2;
102	    case 'up20': return age <= 20 ? Math.atan2(vy, vx) + Math.PI / 2 : rotAcc;
103	    case 'tumble': return rotAcc;
104	    case 'roll': return vx * 0.1;
105	    case 'spin': return rotAcc;
106	    case 'mirror-right': return Math.atan2(vy, vx) + (vx < 0 ? Math.PI : 0);
107	    case 'up-mirror': return Math.atan2(vy, vx) + Math.PI / 2;   // 930 :44846-44849 atan2(−vy,−vx)−π/2 ≡ 同角
108	    case 'upright':
109	    case 'upright-mirror': return 0;
110	    case 'grow': return age * (Math.PI / 300);
111	    default: return 0;
112	  }
113	}
114	
115	export class TownShot extends Entity {
116	  w = 10; h = 10;
117	  vx: number; vy: number;
118	  damage: number;
119	  knockback: number;
120	  projId: number;
121	  life = 600;
122	  dead = false;
123	  /** 弹附加 ai 参数(124/142/227 等特化弹;本仓多数弹不消费,仅存档语义) */
124	  ai1 = 0; ai2 = 0;
125	  /** 588 弹体 timeLeft=480(:54922-54925) */
126	  private lifeOverride?: number;
127	  /** 绘制 scale(SetDefaults;绘制尺寸 = 贴图原生 × scale,与判定盒解耦) */
128	  drawScale = 1;
129	  /** 出世 life(MountShot 不调 super.fixedUpdate,age 用 life 差值推导) */
130	  private age0 = 600;
131	  /** 翻滚/自旋累积角(tumble/up20 后段/spin 模式) */
132	  rotAcc = 0;
133	
134	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, knockback: number, projId: number,
135	    opts?: { ai1?: number; ai2?: number; life?: number; noGravity?: boolean }) {
136	    super();
137	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
138	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
139	    this.damage = damage;
140	    this.knockback = knockback;
141	    this.projId = projId;
142	    if (opts) {
143	      if (opts.ai1 !== undefined) this.ai1 = opts.ai1;
144	      if (opts.ai2 !== undefined) this.ai2 = opts.ai2;
145	      if (opts.life !== undefined) this.lifeOverride = opts.life;
146	      if (opts.noGravity) this.noGrav = true;
147	    }
148	    const def = projectileData(projId);
149	    if (def) { this.w = def.width || 10; this.h = def.height || 10; }
150	    this.drawScale = def?.scale ?? 1;
151	    if (this.lifeOverride !== undefined) this.life = this.lifeOverride;
152	    this.age0 = this.life;
153	    // 589 五列变体:原版出生 ai[1]=rand5(NPC.cs:54953 末参),发射端未传时本地补
154	    if (projId === 589 && (opts?.ai1 === undefined)) this.ai1 = Math.floor(Math.random() * 5);
155	  }
156	
157	  private noGrav = false;
158	
159	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
160	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
161	    if (!ctx) return;
162	    const img = projSprite(this.projId);
163	    const spec = townDrawSpec(this.projId);
164	    const age = this.age0 - this.life;
165	    const fIdx = townFrameIndex(this.projId, age, this.ai1, this.ai2);
166	    ctx.save();
167	    ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
168	    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
169	    const ang = townRotation(spec.rot, this.vx, this.vy, this.rotAcc, age, spec.spinRate);
170	    ctx.rotate(ang);
171	    // 水平镜像族:mirror-right(585 :54715)/up-mirror(930 :44844)/upright-mirror(880 AI_183)
172	    if ((spec.rot === 'mirror-right' || spec.rot === 'up-mirror' || spec.rot === 'upright-mirror')
173	      && this.vx < 0) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
174	    if (img && (img.width > 0) && img.width > 0) {
175	      // 源切片直取(9 参 drawImage,不走 projFrameImg 的 document 依赖):
176	      // 横向条(589/590)切列 / 竖排行(585/880)切行 / 单帧整图(cols=0 勿除零)
177	      const cols = spec.cols;
178	      const rows = cols > 0 ? 1 : projFrameCount(this.projId);
179	      const cw = cols > 0 ? img.width / cols : img.width;
180	      const ch = cols > 0 ? img.height : img.height / rows;
181	      const sx = cols > 0 ? fIdx * cw : 0;
182	      const sy = cols > 0 ? 0 : fIdx * ch;
183	      // 原生帧尺寸 × SetDefaults scale(589 曾整条 100×28 压进 10×10、590 压成 14×2.9)
184	      const s = this.drawScale;
185	      // 586 净化脉冲:scale = age/100 钳 1(AI_111 :38729-38733)
186	      const gs = spec.rot === 'grow' ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0.02, age / 100)) : 1;
187	      const dw = cw * s * gs, dh = ch * s * gs;
188	      ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, cw, ch, -dw / 2, -dh / 2, dw, dh);
189	    } else {
190	      ctx.fillStyle = '#D8C8A0';
191	      ctx.fillRect(-this.w / 2, -this.h / 2, this.w, this.h);
192	    }
193	    ctx.restore();
194	  }
195	
196	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks): void {
197	    if (--this.life <= 0) { this.dead = true; return; }
198	    if (!this.noGrav) this.vy += GRAV;
199	    // 翻滚/自旋累积(aiStyle 2 :21517 / 自旋款)——up20 前 20t 由姿态锁覆盖
200	    const spec = townDrawSpec(this.projId);
201	    const age = this.age0 - this.life;
202	    if (spec.rot === 'tumble' || (spec.rot === 'up20' && age > 20)) {
203	      this.rotAcc += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx >= 0 ? 1 : -1);
204	    } else if (spec.rot === 'spin') {
205	      this.rotAcc += spec.spinRate;
206	    }
207	    this.x += this.vx;
208	    this.y += this.vy;
209	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
210	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
211	    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) { this.dead = true; return; }
212	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
213	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean;
214	        hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks, pen?: number, crit?: boolean, pierce?: number, fromPlayer?: boolean) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
215	      if (e.dead || !projOverlap(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, e)) continue;
216	      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game, 0, false, 0, false)) {
217	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
218	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
219	      }
220	      this.dead = true; // 穿透 1(城镇自卫弹均为 penetrate 1 或 -1 特化弹,近似单发)
221	      return;
222	    }
223	  }
224	}
225	

```

  /** Projectile.Kill() case 669（Projectile.cs:68701-68737,酒馆老板麦酒投掷弹）:
   *  爆音 Item_13 + 白泡 12/金泡 80×2 两档尘 + Resize(144,144) 中心不变 →
   *  判定框交叠玩家/城镇 NPC → AddBuff(25, 3659) Tipsy（玩家走 BuffState,
   *  城镇 NPC 走 applyTipsy 单槽）+ Damage() 144×144 溅射（伤害已在发射端过
   *  num2×难度曲线;pierce 0 免疫帧拦同敌双吃,与原版 localNPCImmunity 同效）。
   *  三死点共用:弹时尽(:68701 前置 life 归零)/撞块(:211)/命中敌(:220) */
  private kill669(game: GameHooks): void {
    const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;
    game.playSfxFiles(['Item_13'], 1, cx, cy);                       // :68702 PlaySound(13)
    // 尘近似两档:白 12(α110 vel×1.5) :68706-68712 / 金 noGravity 160
    // (scale1.2 vel×1.0-7.0 + scale0.8 vel×1.0-4.0) :68713-68723
    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, 'rgba(255,255,255,0.43)', 12, 1.5, { grav: 0.003 });
    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, 'rgba(245,200,30,0.61)', 80, 4, { grav: 0.0005 });
    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, 'rgba(245,200,30,0.61)', 80, 2, { grav: 0.0005 });
    // Resize(144,144)（:68724,中心不变）
    const ex = cx - 72, ey = cy - 72, ER = 144;
    // 玩家入框 → Tipsy 3659t（:68726-68730;BuffState 以秒计 3659/60）
    const p = game.player as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; buffs?: { apply(t: unknown, sec: number): void } } | null;
    if (p && !p.dead && rectOverlap(ex, ey, ER, ER, p.x, p.y, p.w, p.h)) {
      p.buffs?.apply(BuffType.Tipsy, 3659 / 60);
    }
    // 城镇 NPC 入框 → AddBuff(25,3659)（:68731-68734;duck-type 防 HMR fork/环导）
    for (const n of game.npcs?.() ?? []) {
      const tn = n as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; applyTipsy?: (t: number) => void };
      if (!tn || tn.dead || typeof tn.applyTipsy !== 'function') continue;
      if (rectOverlap(ex, ey, ER, ER, tn.x, tn.y, tn.w, tn.h)) tn.applyTipsy(3659);
    }
    // Damage()（:68736）:144×144 内敌怪吃弹伤
    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
      const e = ent as unknown as { ... };
      if (e.dead || !rectOverlap(ex, ey, ER, ER, e.x, e.y, e.w, e.h)) continue;
      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * this.knockback, 0, game, 0, false, 0, false)) {
        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
        game.addDamageNumber(e.x, e.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
      }
    }
  }

  /** DeadCellsPotionStation 在场(:5206 buff 时长×1.2) */
  deadCells = false;
  apply(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
    // buffImmune 门（AddBuff :5052——免疫则直接 return，不掉已有同类 buff）
    const vb = BUFF_DEFS[type]?.vanillaBuff;
    if (vb !== undefined && this.immuneVanilla.has(vb)) return;
    // DeadCellsPotionStation(:5206-5209——buff 1-18,104-117,121-124,257,343 时长 ×1.2)
    if (vb !== undefined && BUFF_TIME_EXTENDED_BY_DEAD_CELLS.has(vb) && durationSec > 0 && this.deadCells) {
      durationSec *= 1.2;
    }
    // D2 专家/大师 debuff 时长缩放（Player.cs:5211-5215+GameDifficultyData.cs:71:
    // BuffTimeIsExtendedWithGameDifficulty {20,22,23,24,323,30,31,32,33,35,36,39,44,324,46,47,69,70,80}
    // → Expert ×2 / Master ×2.5——仅时长,DoT 伤害不缩。2026-08-13）
    if (vb !== undefined && BUFF_TIME_EXTENDED_BY_DIFFICULTY.has(vb) && durationSec > 0) {
      const diff = scaleStatsWorld().difficulty;
      if (diff >= GDL.MASTER) durationSec *= 2.5;
      else if (diff >= GDL.EXPERT) durationSec *= 2;
    }

downedSlimeKing: true } }).downedSlimeKing).toBe(true);\n    // 非链内 Boss（如月总 398/哀木 89 等）不误触\n    const none = townNpcProgressionFromWorld({ flags: { downed_398: true, downed_89: true } });\n    expect(townNpcProgressionBoost(none)).toEqual({ damageMul: 1, chanceMul: 2 });\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// 端到端：发射端结算 num × num2 → 难度曲线（NPC.cs:54897/:55169/:55319/:55466）\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ninterface TestEnemy {\n  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; damage: number;\n  hurt: (d: number) => boolean;\n}\n\nfunction makeGame(opts: {\n  difficulty?: number;\n  flags?: Record<string, boolean>;\n  enemies?: TestEnemy[];\n  npcs?: TownNPC[];\n  player?: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; buffs?: BuffState };\n} = {}) {\n  const world = new World(200, 200, 100, 'town-prog-test');\n  world.difficulty = opts.difficulty ?? 0;\n  Object.assign(world.flags, opts.flags ?? {});\n  for (let x = 20; x <= 180; x++) world.store.setTileSilent(x, 100, 1);\n  const shots: TownShot[] = [];\n  const sounds: string[] = [];\n  const enemies = opts.enemies ?? [];\n  const npcs = opts.npcs ?? [];\n  const enemyHurt: number[] = [];\n  return {\n    world, shots, sounds, enemyHurt, npcs,\n    game: {\n      world,\n      player: opts.player ?? { x: -9999, y: -9999, w: 0, h: 0, dead: true },\n      enemies: () => enemies,\n      npcs: () => npcs,\n      spawnTownShot: (s: TownShot) => shots.push(s),\n      spawnParticles: () => {}, playSfx: () => {},\n      playSfxFiles: (files: string[]) => sounds.push(...files),\n      addDamageNumber: () => {},\n      critters: () => [],\n    } as never,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction runUntil(npc: TownNPC, game: unknown, cond: () => boolean, maxT = 2000): void {\n  for (let t = 0; t < maxT && !cond(); t++) npc.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game as never);\n}\n\ndescribe('发射端消费 num2（shootAt/swingUpdate）', () => {\n  it('酒保(550)近战弹 669：基线 24 / downed_4 后 trunc(24×1.05)=25', () => {\n    for (const [flags, want] of [\n      [{}, 24],\n      [{ downed_4: true }, 25],\n    ] as const) {\n      const keep = new TownNPC('tavernkeep', 100 * 16, 99 * 16);\n      const enemy: TestEnemy = {\n        x: 102 * 16, y: 98 * 16, w: 20, h: 40, dead: false, damage: 40, hurt: () => true,\n      };\n      const { game, shots } = makeGame({ flags: flags as Record<string, boolean>, enemies: [enemy] });\n      runUntil(keep, game, () => shots.length > 0);\n      expect(shots.length, JSON.stringify(flags)).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n      expect(shots[0].projId).toBe(669);\n      expect(shots[0].damage).toBe(want);\n    }\n  });\n\n  it('酒保(550)+硬模式+Expert：trunc(24×1.4×1.5)=50（num2 先于难度曲线）', () => {\n    const keep = new TownNPC('tavernkeep', 100 * 16, 99 * 16);\n    const enemy: TestEnemy = {\n      x: 102 * 16, y: 98 * 16, w: 20, h: 40, dead: false, damage: 40, hurt: () => true,\n    };\n    const { game, shots } = makeGame({ difficulty: 1, flags: { hardMode: true }, enemies: [enemy] });\n    runUntil(keep, game, () => shots.length > 0);\n    expect(shots[0].damage).toBe(Math.trunc(24 * 1.4 * 1.5));   // 50\n  });\n\n  it('发型师(353)挥击态：硬模式 hurt 收到 trunc(10×1.4)=14（:55466 同式）', () => {\n    const stylist = new TownNPC('stylist', 100 * 16, 99 * 16);\n    const got: number[] = [];\n    const enemy: TestEnemy = {\n      x: 100 * 16 + 26, y: 99 * 16 + 8, w: 20, h: 40, dead: false, damage: 40,\n      hurt: (d) => { got.push(d); return true; },\n    };\n    const { game } = makeGame({ flags: { hardMode: true }, enemies: [enemy] });\n    runUntil(stylist, game, () => got.length > 0);\n    expect(got.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);\n    expect(got[0]).toBe(14);\n  });\n\n  it('tipsy 实例位：applyTipsy 后弹伤 ×1.1（trunc(24×1.1)=26）,衰减尽回基线', () => {\n    const keep = new TownNPC('tavernkeep', 100 * 16, 99 * 16);\n    const enemy: TestEnemy = {\n      x: 102 * 16, y: 98 * 16, w: 20, h: 40, dead: false, damage: 40, hurt: () => true,\n    };\n    const { game, shots } = makeGame({ enemies: [enemy] });\n    keep.applyTipsy(600);                       // buff 25 在列 → NPC.tipsy\n    runUntil(keep, game, () => shots.length > 0);\n    expect(shots[0].damage).toBe(26);\n    // 快照语义：剩余 1t 的最后一 tick 仍 true（:93174 递减后置位）\n    const tail = new TownNPC('tavernkeep', 100 * 16, 99 * 16);\n    tail.applyTipsy(1);\n    tail.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, makeGame({}).game);\n    expect(tail.tipsy).toBe(true);\n    tail.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, makeGame({}).game);\n    expect(tail.tipsy).toBe(false);             // :93333 重置\n    // 衰减尽 → 伤害回基线\n    const sober = new TownNPC('tavernkeep', 100 * 16, 99 * 16);\n    const { game: g2, shots: s2 } = makeGame({ enemies: [{\n      x: 102 * 16, y: 98 * 16, w: 20, h: 40, dead: false, damage: 40, hurt: () => true,\n    }] });\n    sober.applyTipsy(2);\n    sober.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, g2);\n    sober.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, g2);\n    expect(sober.tipsy).toBe(false);\n    runUntil(sober, g2, () => s2.length > 0);\n    expect(s2[0].damage).toBe(24);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('麦酒弹 669 Kill 爆炸（Projectile.cs:68701-68737,tipsy 施加链）', () => {\n  it('落地爆炸：框内玩家+城镇 NPC 入 Tipsy 3659t / 框外 NPC 不中 / 框内敌怪溅射', () => {\n    const inBox = new TownNPC('guide', 1650, 1540);        // 爆炸框内（144×144）\n    const outBox = new TownNPC('guide', 1584, 1200);       // 框外 300px 上方\n    const player = { x: 1560, y: 1550, w: 20, h: 42, dead: false, buffs: new BuffState() };\n    const got: number[] = [];\n    const enemy: TestEnemy = {\n      x: 1540, y: 1570, w: 20, h: 40, dead: false, damage: 40,\n      hurt: (d) => { got.push(d); return true; },\n    };\n    const { game, sounds } = makeGame({\n      enemies: [enemy], npcs: [inBox, outBox], player,\n    });\n    // 直投:自由落体 17t 内触地 → tile 死点 → kill669\n    const shot = new TownShot(1584, 1552, 0, 0, 24, 9, 669, {});\n    for (let t = 0; t < 40 && !shot.dead; t++) shot.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n    expect(shot.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(sounds).toContain('Item_13');                   // PlaySound(13)\n    expect(inBox.tipsyT).toBe(3659);                       // AddBuff(25, 3659)\n    expect(outBox.tipsyT).toBe(0);                         // 框外不中\n    expect(player.buffs.has(BuffType.Tipsy)).toBe(true);   // 玩家入框\n    expect(got).toContain(24);                             // Damage() 溅射\n  });\n\n  it('弹时尽死点同样触发爆炸（life 归零 Kill 路径）', () => {\n    const npc = new TownNPC('guide', 1584, 1560);\n    const { game } = makeGame({ npcs: [npc] });\n    const shot = new TownShot(1584, 1552, 0, 0, 24, 9, 669, { life: 1 });\n    shot.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, game);\n    expect(shot.dead).toBe(true);\n    expect(npc.tipsyT).toBe(3659);\n  });\n});\n"
}

export function startHardmode(world: World): { wasHard: boolean } {
  const wasHard = !!world.flags.hardMode;
  (world.flags as Record<string, boolean>).hardMode = true;
  initializeHardMode(world, new RNG(world.seed));
  return { wasHard };
}

```
